<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-50180873" data-id="50180873" data-tags="Programming|Security|Code" data-score="669" data-comments="264" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180873" data-time="1792590333">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/php/php-src/releases/tag/php-8.2.35" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PHP 8.2.35</span><span class='zh'>PHP 8.2.35 发布</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PHP 8.2.35 is a maintenance release that patches several security and stability issues in the 8.2 line.</span><span class='zh'>这是对 8.2 分支的维护性更新，包含若干安全修复与错误修正。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PHP 8.2.35 is a maintenance release that patches several security and stability issues in the 8.2 line. It’s a standard part of the PHP patch cycle, and upgrading helps close vulnerabilities and improve reliability. If you run PHP 8.2 in production, apply the update to stay supported and secure.</span><span class='zh'>这是对 8.2 分支的维护性更新，包含若干安全修复与错误修正。强烈建议在生产环境尽快升级，以降低潜在漏洞并提升稳定性。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techdirt.com/2026/10/20/accident-or-not-the-no-kings-takedown-is-why-you-shouldnt-rent-your-megaphone-from-zuck/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The No Kings Takedown Is Why You Shouldn&#x27;t Rent Your Megaphone from Zuck</span><span class='zh'>No Kings Takedown：你不该把扩音器租给扎克伯格</span></a><span class="dom">techdirt.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TechDirt&#x27;s piece argues that relying on a single gatekeeper for reach—like Facebook&#x27;s megaphone—is risky.</span><span class='zh'>TechDirt 讨论 No Kings takedown，强调别把话语权交给单一平台（如 Zuckerberg 的平台）可能带来的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TechDirt&#x27;s piece argues that relying on a single gatekeeper for reach—like Facebook&#x27;s megaphone—is risky. It uses the No Kings takedown to illustrate how platform power can backfire on users and why diverse channels matter.</span><span class='zh'>TechDirt 讨论 No Kings takedown，强调别把话语权交给单一平台（如 Zuckerberg 的平台）可能带来的风险。该事件凸显平台垄断力量对创作者的潜在打压，强调多渠道传播的重要性。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wiz.io/blog/php2shell-php-rce" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PHP2Shell: AI uncovers critical RCE in PHP</span><span class='zh'>PHP2Shell：AI 发现 PHP 的关键远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">wiz.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI-driven tool PHP2Shell uncovers a critical PHP RCE vulnerability, illustrating how AI can accelerate security research and the importance of secure coding practices.</span><span class='zh'>AI 驱动的工具 PHP2Shell 发现了一个严重的 PHP 远程执行漏洞，展示了 AI 在安全研究中的潜力与风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI-driven tool PHP2Shell uncovers a critical PHP RCE vulnerability, illustrating how AI can accelerate security research and the importance of secure coding practices. The finding underscores the need for rigorous input validation and timely patching of PHP deployments.</span><span class='zh'>AI 驱动的工具 PHP2Shell 发现了一个严重的 PHP 远程执行漏洞，展示了 AI 在安全研究中的潜力与风险。此发现强调对 PHP 部署进行严格的输入校验与快速修补的重要性。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>18.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>363</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180836' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>121</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50180943" data-id="50180943" data-tags="Programming|Security" data-score="275" data-comments="119" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180943" data-time="1792590605">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.php.net/index.php" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PHP 8.5.12, 8.4.27, 8.3.36 and 8.2.35 out</span><span class='zh'>PHP 8.5.12、8.4.27、8.3.36 和 8.2.35 发布</span></a><span class="dom">php.net</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Updates across four PHP branches—8.5.12, 8.4.27, 8.3.36, and 8.2.35—ship with security fixes and bug fixes. Administrators should plan upgrades to mitigate vulnerabilities and improve stability across deployments.</span><span class='zh'>同时发布了四个 PHP 版本分支的更新，包含安全和错误修正。建议管理员安排升级以降低漏洞风险并提升部署的稳定性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://scotthelme.co.uk/eight-bits-of-trust-emudhras-ca-keys-cracked-in-milliseconds/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ee/88/ee889f88-37ef-43e5-9180-f9b88ee6261d/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/emudhra.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='scotthelme.co.uk' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scotthelme.co.uk/eight-bits-of-trust-emudhras-ca-keys-cracked-in-milliseconds/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Eight Bits of Trust; eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys cracked in milliseconds</span><span class='zh'>信任的八位字节：eMudhra 的 CA 密钥在毫秒内被破解</span></a><span class="dom">scotthelme.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate that eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys can be cracked in milliseconds, challenging TLS trust assumptions.</span><span class='zh'>研究者显示 eMudhra 的 CA 钥匙可在毫秒内被破解，挑战了 TLS 信任的基本假设。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate that eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys can be cracked in milliseconds, challenging TLS trust assumptions. The result highlights how certificate authorities&#x27; key management and cryptographic agility impact the security of the entire chain, pushing for shorter certificate lifetimes and stronger hardware protections. It also underscores the need for tougher CA audits and improved trust-chain resilience.</span><span class='zh'>研究者显示 eMudhra 的 CA 钥匙可在毫秒内被破解，挑战了 TLS 信任的基本假设。此现象突显证书颁发机构在密钥管理与加密灵活性方面对整个信任链安全的影响，推动对更短证书有效期和更强硬件保护的需求，同时强调对 CA 审计和信任链韧性的改进。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>10.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>608</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177916' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>267</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50177911" data-id="50177911" data-tags="Science|Energy" data-score="572" data-comments="690" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177911" data-time="1792573669">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://unric.org/en/unep-emissions-gap-report-2026-1-5-degree-overshoot/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The 1.5°C goal of the Paris Agreement is no longer viable</span><span class='zh'>巴黎协定的1.5°C目标不再可行</span></a><span class="dom">unric.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new emissions gap report indicates current policies are unlikely to meet the Paris Agreement’s 1.5°C target.</span><span class='zh'>最新的减排差距报告显示，现有政策难以实现巴黎协定的1.5°C目标。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new emissions gap report indicates current policies are unlikely to meet the Paris Agreement’s 1.5°C target. Without bolder decarbonization, we risk overshooting and facing harsher climate impacts; tech implications point to rapid energy transition, efficiency gains, and scalable negative-emissions solutions.</span><span class='zh'>最新的减排差距报告显示，现有政策难以实现巴黎协定的1.5°C目标。如果不加速减排，可能超出目标并带来更严重的气候影响；对科技的启示包括快速推进能源转型、提升能效，以及扩大负排放技术的应用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A post claims the Dutch National Cyber Security Center went offline because of PHP, fueling a broader debate about language choices for critical infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>有帖文称荷兰国家网络安全中心因使用 PHP 而离线，点燃了对关键基础设施语言选择的辩论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A post claims the Dutch National Cyber Security Center went offline because of PHP, fueling a broader debate about language choices for critical infrastructure. The claim highlights security and reliability questions around using PHP in national-grade systems.</span><span class='zh'>有帖文称荷兰国家网络安全中心因使用 PHP 而离线，点燃了对关键基础设施语言选择的辩论。此说法突显在国家级系统中使用 PHP 所带来的安全性与可靠性疑问。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwen3-Coder outputs less secure code when asked to adopt a US persona, revealing how persona constraints can impact security; emphasizes need for safeguards and cross-checks in AI coding tools.</span><span class='zh'>当被要求按美国人设输出代码时，Qwen3-Coder 的代码安全性下降，揭示角色设定可能对安全性造成负面影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Qwen3-Coder outputs less secure code when asked to adopt a US persona, revealing how persona constraints can impact security; emphasizes need for safeguards and cross-checks in AI coding tools.</span><span class='zh'>当被要求按美国人设输出代码时，Qwen3-Coder 的代码安全性下降，揭示角色设定可能对安全性造成负面影响。强调需要对 AI 编码工具加入保护措施与交叉检查。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PHP has announced an upcoming critical security release to patch multiple high-risk vulnerabilities, including potential remote code execution vectors.</span><span class='zh'>PHP 公布将发布一轮关键安全更新，修补多处高危险漏洞，可能含远程代码执行向量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PHP has announced an upcoming critical security release to patch multiple high-risk vulnerabilities, including potential remote code execution vectors. Admins should plan to apply the update promptly to mitigate exposure in PHP-based apps, underscoring the ongoing security risk in the PHP ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>PHP 公布将发布一轮关键安全更新，修补多处高危险漏洞，可能含远程代码执行向量。管理员应尽早应用该更新以降低基于 PHP 的应用暴露风险，凸显 PHP 生态持续的安全挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepInfer is a new high-performance inference framework designed for DeepSeek models, aiming to maximize throughput on modern hardware.</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer 是面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架，旨在于现代硬件上实现最大的吞吐量。</span></p>

It emphasizes modular backends and optimized runtimes to speed up deployment of large-scale AI.</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer 是面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架，旨在于现代硬件上实现最大的吞吐量。它通过模块化后端与优化运行时来提升大规模 AI 推理的部署效率。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.joshwcomeau.com/css/interop-2027/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/images/og-interop-2027.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='joshwcomeau.com' data-c='#f472b6' data-tag='Design'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.joshwcomeau.com/css/interop-2027/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Future of CSS</span><span class='zh'>CSS 的未来</span></a><span class="dom">joshwcomeau.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CSS is evolving toward stronger cross‑browser interoperability and more programmable styling.</span><span class='zh'>CSS 将在跨浏览器互操作性和可编程样式方面变得更强大。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CSS is evolving toward stronger cross‑browser interoperability and more programmable styling. Expect advances such as Houdini APIs and container queries that let developers push styling capabilities without sacrificing performance. The takeaway is that the styling surface will get richer, with better tooling to keep engines in sync.</span><span class='zh'>CSS 将在跨浏览器互操作性和可编程样式方面变得更强大。预计会有 Houdini API、容器查询等新特性，帮助开发者在不牺牲性能的前提下扩展样式能力。核心要点是样式体系将更丰富，工具链与引擎之间的协作也将同步提升。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful project adds permit bureaucracy to a SimCity-like environment, showing how permit delays shape growth, zoning decisions, and development timelines.</span><span class='zh'>一个将许可制度引入类似 SimCity 的模拟环境的有趣项目，展示许可拖延如何影响城市增长、分区决策与开发节奏。</span></p>

The result offers a tangible look at how administrative friction can influence urban planning experiments and policy outcomes.</span><span class='zh'>一个将许可制度引入类似 SimCity 的模拟环境的有趣项目，展示许可拖延如何影响城市增长、分区决策与开发节奏。该成果为官僚摩擦如何影响城市规划实验与政策结果提供了直观的观察。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>eMudhra intermediate CA compromise</span><span class='zh'>EMudhra 中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协</span></a><span class="dom">emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EMudhra reports compromise of its Intermediate Certificate Authority, potentially enabling issuance of fraudulent certificates.</span><span class='zh'>EMudhra 报告其中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协，可能导致伪造证书的签发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EMudhra reports compromise of its Intermediate Certificate Authority, potentially enabling issuance of fraudulent certificates. The incident highlights PKI supply-chain risk and the need for aggressive revocation, transparency, and improved security controls.</span><span class='zh'>EMudhra 报告其中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协，可能导致伪造证书的签发。这一事件凸显公钥基础设施供应链风险，以及需要更快速的撤销、透明披露和改进的安全控制措施。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>1157</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166579' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>444</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50168396" data-id="50168396" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="748" data-comments="194" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50168396" data-time="1792512127">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mistral.ai/news/voxtral-3/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Voxtral 3</span><span class='zh'>Voxtral 3 上线</span></a><span class="dom">mistral.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mistral AI unveils Voxtral 3, the latest entry in its Voxtral family.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral AI 发布 Voxtral 3，是 Voxtral 系列的最新版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mistral AI unveils Voxtral 3, the latest entry in its Voxtral family. The release signals continued momentum in open-weight models and the trend toward more capable, accessible AI cores.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral AI 发布 Voxtral 3，是 Voxtral 系列的最新版本。该发布体现了开源权重模型的持续发展，以及更强大、易接入的 AI 核心的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/10/meta-muse-exposes-users-personal-and-business-secrets-when-they-share-conversations/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Muse exposes users&#x27; personal and business secrets when they share conversations</span><span class='zh'>Muse 在共享对话时暴露用户的个人与商业秘密</span></a><span class="dom">arstechnica.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Muse reportedly exposes sensitive personal and business secrets when conversations are shared, highlighting a privacy risk in collaborative AI tools.</span><span class='zh'>Muse 在共享对话时暴露了用户的个人和商业秘密，凸显协作型 AI 工具中的隐私风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Muse reportedly exposes sensitive personal and business secrets when conversations are shared, highlighting a privacy risk in collaborative AI tools. The issue underscores the need for stronger data handling and access controls in shared contexts. Enterprises using such tools should re-evaluate data exposure surfaces and mitigate accordingly.</span><span class='zh'>Muse 在共享对话时暴露了用户的个人和商业秘密，凸显协作型 AI 工具中的隐私风险。问题强调需要在共享场景中加强数据处理与访问控制。使用此类工具的企业应重新评估数据暴露面并采取缓解措施。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An intimate look at the challenge of tapering off benzodiazepines, highlighting withdrawal symptoms, relapse risk, and the gaps in care for people seeking to discontinue.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章用个人视角揭示戒除苯二氮卓类药物的艰难过程，强调戒断症状、复发风险，以及医疗体系在扶持中的不足。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An intimate look at the challenge of tapering off benzodiazepines, highlighting withdrawal symptoms, relapse risk, and the gaps in care for people seeking to discontinue. The piece reframes prescription dependence as a broader public-health issue, urging safer tapering, patient-centered support, and better clinician guidance.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章用个人视角揭示戒除苯二氮卓类药物的艰难过程，强调戒断症状、复发风险，以及医疗体系在扶持中的不足。它把处方药依赖看作广泛的公共卫生议题，呼吁更安全的减药路径、以患者为中心的支持，以及更清晰的临床指引。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Facebook is gating link posting on Pages behind a paid subscription, limiting to two links per month.</span><span class='zh'>Facebook 对 Pages 的链接发布实施付费门槛，每月仅限发布两条链接，超过部分需订阅。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Facebook is gating link posting on Pages behind a paid subscription, limiting to two links per month. This marks a shift toward subscription-based reach for Page administrators. It raises concerns for marketers and developers who rely on organic link sharing.</span><span class='zh'>Facebook 对 Pages 的链接发布实施付费门槛，每月仅限发布两条链接，超过部分需订阅。此举体现了平台对可变盈利模式的推动，也可能影响依赖页面曝光的营销和开发者。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/10/feds-suspect-ransom-negotiator-ran-shinyhunters-extortion-site/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Feds suspect ransom negotiator ran ShinyHunters extortion site</span><span class='zh'>联邦调查人员怀疑勒索谈判者运营 ShinyHunters 的勒索网站</span></a><span class="dom">krebsonsecurity.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>US prosecutors allege a ransom negotiator ran the extortion site used by the ShinyHunters gang, acting as the broker between victims and attackers.</span><span class='zh'>美国检方指控一名勒索谈判人运营被 ShinyHunters 用来施压受害者的勒索网站，充当攻击者与受害者之间的中介。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>US prosecutors allege a ransom negotiator ran the extortion site used by the ShinyHunters gang, acting as the broker between victims and attackers. If true, the case exposes a monetization choke point in cybercrime and could shape how authorities disrupt extortion ecosystems and pursue suspects. It underscores how sophisticated threat actors rely on intermediaries to pressure victims and extract payments.</span><span class='zh'>美国检方指控一名勒索谈判人运营被 ShinyHunters 用来施压受害者的勒索网站，充当攻击者与受害者之间的中介。若指控成立，这一角色揭示了网络犯罪生态中的货币化环节，可能影响执法机构打击勒索网络的策略。事件凸显威胁行为体对中间人依赖以推动敲诈和索取赎金的作业流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-pytorch/torch_tpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch back end for TPUs</span><span class='zh'>Google 开源 TorchTPU：面向 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend for TPUs, making TPUs feel like a first-class citizen for PyTorch users.</span><span class='zh'>Google 将 TorchTPU 开源，提供对 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端，使 PyTorch 用户能够直接在 TPUs 上运行模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend for TPUs, making TPUs feel like a first-class citizen for PyTorch users. This lowers the barrier to running PyTorch models on Google&#x27;s accelerators and could shift performance comparisons across ML stacks.</span><span class='zh'>Google 将 TorchTPU 开源，提供对 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端，使 PyTorch 用户能够直接在 TPUs 上运行模型。此举降低了在 TPU 上运行 PyTorch 的门槛，可能改变不同 ML 堆栈间的性能对比。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In a high-profile regulatory dispute, Ofcom concedes defeat in a case involving 4chan, underscoring the limits of national regulators to police global platforms.</span><span class='zh'>在一起备受关注的监管案件中，Ofcom 就 4chan 案件认输，凸显英国监管机构对全球性平台治理的有限能力。</span></p>

The outcome may recalibrate expectations for cross-border moderation obligations.</span><span class='zh'>在一起备受关注的监管案件中，Ofcom 就 4chan 案件认输，凸显英国监管机构对全球性平台治理的有限能力。此结果可能重新校准对跨境内容治理的期待。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/typescript-rust-port/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tsrs: A Port of TypeScript to Rust</span><span class='zh'>Tsrs：TypeScript 到 Rust 的移植</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tsrs is a port of TypeScript tooling to Rust, enabling TypeScript-like workflows to run on a Rust-based runtime.</span><span class='zh'>Tsrs 将 TypeScript 的工具链移植到 Rust，使 TypeScript 工作流能够在 Rust 运行时上运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tsrs is a port of TypeScript tooling to Rust, enabling TypeScript-like workflows to run on a Rust-based runtime. The project promises safer memory guarantees and potential performance gains, inviting developers to re-evaluate toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>Tsrs 将 TypeScript 的工具链移植到 Rust，使 TypeScript 工作流能够在 Rust 运行时上运行。该项目可能带来更安全的内存管理与潜在的性能提升，促使开发者重新评估工具链选择。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A tactic is described where fake statistics are published to be cited by large language models, illustrating how misinformation can leak into AI training data.</span><span class='zh'>文中描述了一种策略：发布伪造的统计数据以被大型语言模型引用，揭示了错误信息如何进入 AI 训练数据并影响模型输出。文中讨论了来源可追溯性、防护措施，以及当虚假数据被当作事实时对模型可靠性的风险。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MicroG offers a libre reimplementation of Google Mobile Services, enabling Android runs without proprietary Google binaries.</span><span class='zh'>MicroG 提供对 Google 移动服务的自由开源再实现，使用户在不使用官方谷歌二进制的情况下运行 Android。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MicroG offers a libre reimplementation of Google Mobile Services, enabling Android runs without proprietary Google binaries. It supports many apps and services while prioritizing privacy, empowering custom ROMs and privacy-focused devices, though some apps may rely on proprietary features.</span><span class='zh'>MicroG 提供对 Google 移动服务的自由开源再实现，使用户在不使用官方谷歌二进制的情况下运行 Android。尽管兼容性可能有限，但它为隐私友好的设备与自定义 ROM 提供了可行选项，某些应用仍可能依赖专有特性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenBSD 8.0 arrives as the newest stable release in the security-focused family, pushing forward on correctness and minimal attack surface. It brings base-system updates, improved hardware support, and ongoing hardening features that appeal to sysadmins who prioritize reliability and security.</span><span class='zh'>OpenBSD 8.0 作为该安全优先发行线的最新稳定版本，继续强调正确性与较小的攻击面。该版本包含基线系统更新、对更多硬件的支持提升，以及持续的安全强化，适合追求高可靠性和稳健性的系统管理员。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ipinfo.io/blog/france-fridges-hack" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>France&#x27;s supermarket fridges were vulnerable online for months</span><span class='zh'>法国超市冷藏柜数月暴露在网上存在漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">ipinfo.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Security researchers found that connected fridge units in French supermarkets had misconfigurations or insecure access exposed online for months, enabling potential manipulation of inventory data or remote control.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员发现法国多家超市的连网冷柜数月暴露在公开网络中，可能被未经授权的访问者远程操控或篡改库存数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Security researchers found that connected fridge units in French supermarkets had misconfigurations or insecure access exposed online for months, enabling potential manipulation of inventory data or remote control. The incident underscores how consumer hardware in retail can be entry points for cyberattacks, and highlights need for better IoT management and firmware hygiene.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员发现法国多家超市的连网冷柜数月暴露在公开网络中，可能被未经授权的访问者远程操控或篡改库存数据。事件暴露零售业物联网设备的安全薄弱点，也凸显加强设备管理、固件更新和网络分段的重要性。</span></p>

Patch PHP to affected versions and apply recommended mitigations.</span><span class='zh'>PHP session.upload_progress 的堆上使用后释放漏洞（CVE-2026-109618/109619）可能导致崩溃或远程代码执行。请升级到受影响版本并应用推荐的缓解措施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://unsung.aresluna.org/good-type-against-all-odds/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Good type against all odds</span><span class='zh'>逆境中的好类型</span></a><span class="dom">unsung.aresluna.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An argument for the value of good typing, arguing that strong type systems catch errors earlier, improve maintainability, and support safer refactoring as codebases grow.</span><span class='zh'>主张强类型的价值，认为强类型系统能更早发现错误、提升可维护性、并在代码库扩大时支持更安全的重构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An argument for the value of good typing, arguing that strong type systems catch errors earlier, improve maintainability, and support safer refactoring as codebases grow. The piece defends typing against trends toward untyped or dynamic approaches by showing practical gains. In other words, type discipline remains a productivity booster, not a luxury.</span><span class='zh'>主张强类型的价值，认为强类型系统能更早发现错误、提升可维护性、并在代码库扩大时支持更安全的重构。文章通过实际收益捍卫类型系统，反对向无类型或动态语言的趋势。总之，类型规范被视为提高生产力的关键工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pillowfort.social/posts/8413273" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pillowfort Is Closing</span><span class='zh'>Pillowfort 将关闭</span></a><span class="dom">pillowfort.social</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pillowfort is shutting down, prompting users to export data and consider alternatives.</span><span class='zh'>Pillowfort 将关闭，要求用户导出数据并考虑替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pillowfort is shutting down, prompting users to export data and consider alternatives. The move underscores the fragility of independent social platforms and the importance of data portability for communities. It also raises questions about how niche networks can sustain themselves long term.</span><span class='zh'>Pillowfort 将关闭，要求用户导出数据并考虑替代方案。这一举措凸显独立社交平台的脆弱性，以及数据可移植性对社区的重要性。与此同时，也引发了小众网络长期可持续发展的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.quantamagazine.org/in-hilbert-space-all-things-are-quantumly-possible-20260826/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>In Hilbert space, all things are quantumly possible</span><span class='zh'>在希尔伯特空间中，万事皆可量子化</span></a><span class="dom">quantamagazine.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hilbert space underpins the mathematics of quantum possibility, linking abstract functional analysis to what quantum computers might achieve.</span><span class='zh'>希尔伯特空间构成了量子可能性的数学基础，将抽象的函数分析与量子计算可能达到的目标联系起来。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hilbert space underpins the mathematics of quantum possibility, linking abstract functional analysis to what quantum computers might achieve. The piece explains how linear algebra concepts like superposition and interference drive quantum algorithms and why some problems become tractable on quantum hardware. It helps technologists gauge where quantum advantage could matter.</span><span class='zh'>希尔伯特空间构成了量子可能性的数学基础，将抽象的函数分析与量子计算可能达到的目标联系起来。文章阐述了线性代数中的叠加、干涉等概念如何推动量子算法，以及为何某些问题在量子硬件上变得可解。它帮助技术人员判断在哪些场景量子优势才会显现。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Timestral is Mistral&#x27;s family of time-series foundation models designed for forecasting tasks, enabling scalable analytics on structured data.</span><span class='zh'>Timestral 是 Mistral 面向时间序列的基础模型家族，专注于预测任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Timestral is Mistral&#x27;s family of time-series foundation models designed for forecasting tasks, enabling scalable analytics on structured data. By specializing in temporal patterns, these models aim to outperform generic LLMs in finance, IoT, and operations.</span><span class='zh'>Timestral 是 Mistral 面向时间序列的基础模型家族，专注于预测任务。通过对时间模式的建模，这类模型为金融、物联网与运营数据的分析提供可扩展的 AI 解决方案，可能在某些领域优于通用 LLM。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.php.net/archive/2026.php" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Upcoming Critical PHP Security Release</span><span class='zh'>即将发布的关键 PHP 安全更新</span></a><span class="dom">php.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PHP has announced an upcoming critical security release to address multiple high-severity vulnerabilities, including possible remote code execution scenarios.</span><span class='zh'>PHP 发布即将到来的关键安全更新，修补多项高危漏洞，可能涉及远程代码执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PHP has announced an upcoming critical security release to address multiple high-severity vulnerabilities, including possible remote code execution scenarios. Web apps running PHP should prepare to patch promptly to reduce exposure, as unpatched sites remain a common attack vector.</span><span class='zh'>PHP 发布即将到来的关键安全更新，修补多项高危漏洞，可能涉及远程代码执行。运行 PHP 的网站应尽快应用补丁，以降低被利用的风险，因为未打补丁的站点仍然是常见的攻击向量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bolkunov.com/p/on-ai-writing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>On AI writing</span><span class='zh'>论人工智能写作</span></a><span class="dom">bolkunov.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A concise reflection on AI-assisted writing, examining how automation reshapes authorship, workflows, and quality.</span><span class='zh'>对人工智能辅助写作的简要反思，探讨自动化如何改变作者身份、工作流程与文本质量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A concise reflection on AI-assisted writing, examining how automation reshapes authorship, workflows, and quality. It argues for balancing automation with human judgment to preserve nuance and accountability in content creation.</span><span class='zh'>对人工智能辅助写作的简要反思，探讨自动化如何改变作者身份、工作流程与文本质量。主张在自动化与人类判断之间取得平衡，以保留文本的细微表达与创作责任。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nbcnews.com/world/ukraine/ukraine-first-drone-evacuation-wounded-soldier-frontlines-hexacopter-rcna604310" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ukraine claims first drone evacuation of wounded soldier</span><span class='zh'>乌克兰宣布首次用无人机从前线撤离受伤士兵</span></a><span class="dom">nbcnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ukraine reports the first drone-assisted evacuation of a wounded soldier from the front lines, signaling a potential new use case for unmanned aircraft in casualty extraction under combat conditions.</span><span class='zh'>乌克兰声称在前线首次实现受伤士兵的无人机撤离，标志着无人机在战场救援中的新应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ukraine reports the first drone-assisted evacuation of a wounded soldier from the front lines, signaling a potential new use case for unmanned aircraft in casualty extraction under combat conditions. While not replacing medevac helicopters, it could speed triage, reduce risk to human responders, and influence battlefield logistics and tech procurement.</span><span class='zh'>乌克兰声称在前线首次实现受伤士兵的无人机撤离，标志着无人机在战场救援中的新应用。该做法并非取代直升机医撤，但可能加速分级救治、降低人类救援人员暴露风险，并影响未来的战场后勤与相关技术采购。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study benchmarks how current bot-detection systems perform against sophisticated AI agents in 2026, revealing evolving evasion techniques and mixed detector effectiveness.</span><span class='zh'>一项研究基准测试了当前的机器人检测系统在对抗2026年先进 AI 代理时的表现，揭示了不断进化的规避手段与检测器效果的差异性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study benchmarks how current bot-detection systems perform against sophisticated AI agents in 2026, revealing evolving evasion techniques and mixed detector effectiveness. The findings underscore the need for adaptive, multi-layered defenses in moderation and security tooling.</span><span class='zh'>一项研究基准测试了当前的机器人检测系统在对抗2026年先进 AI 代理时的表现，揭示了不断进化的规避手段与检测器效果的差异性。研究结果强调在 moderation 与安全工具中需要更具适应性的多层防御。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nordwand Mono is a new monospaced font designed for coders and adventurers, aiming for high legibility and distinctive personality in IDEs and terminals. It joins a growing ecosystem of expressive, practical coding typefaces that optimize readability during long sessions.</span><span class='zh'>Nordwand Mono 是一款面向程序员与爱好者的新型等宽字体，强调在集成开发环境和终端中的高可读性与独特风格。它加入了注重可读性和个性表达的编码字体家族，适合长时间编码时使用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bsd.network/@brynet/117407035545900963" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenBSD 8.0 release will have inteldrm/radeondrm/amdgpu drivers from Linux 6.18</span><span class='zh'>OpenBSD 8.0 将集成 Linux 6.18 的 inteldrm/radeondrm/amdgpu 驱动</span></a><span class="dom">bsd.network</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenBSD 8.0 will import Linux 6.18 GPU drivers (inteldrm, radeondrm, amdgpu), expanding support for Intel/AMD GPUs.</span><span class='zh'>OpenBSD 8.0 将把 Linux 6.18 的 GPU 驱动（inteldrm、radeondrm、amdgpu）引入到自身代码库中，扩大对英特尔/AMD 显卡的支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenBSD 8.0 will import Linux 6.18 GPU drivers (inteldrm, radeondrm, amdgpu), expanding support for Intel/AMD GPUs. This move improves hardware compatibility and potentially performance on OpenBSD, but raises questions about Linux code dependencies, licensing, and security boundaries.</span><span class='zh'>OpenBSD 8.0 将把 Linux 6.18 的 GPU 驱动（inteldrm、radeondrm、amdgpu）引入到自身代码库中，扩大对英特尔/AMD 显卡的支持。这一举措提升了硬件兼容性与潜在性能，但也带来对 Linux 代码依赖、许可证与安全边界的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/82345" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Go will reject certificate chains containing eight distrusted SPKIs</span><span class='zh'>Go 将拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Go is tightening TLS validation by rejecting certificate chains that include eight distrusted SPKI entries.</span><span class='zh'>Go 将通过在 TLS 验证中拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链，来收紧安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Go is tightening TLS validation by rejecting certificate chains that include eight distrusted SPKI entries. The change reduces attack surface from compromised keys and misissued certificates, nudging developers to audit complex chains or intermediates.</span><span class='zh'>Go 将通过在 TLS 验证中拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链，来收紧安全性。此举降低了被入侵密钥和被篡改证书带来的风险，促使开发者审查包含多中间证书的证书链。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://chriskiehl.com/article/it-is-still-ok-to-enjoy-programming" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>It&#x27;s still ok to enjoy programming</span><span class='zh'>仍然可以享受编程</span></a><span class="dom">chriskiehl.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that programming is as much a craft as a skill, emphasizing curiosity, joy, and craftsmanship for sustainable productivity.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张编程不仅是技能，更是一门工艺；强调好奇心、乐趣和匠心对于长期产出的重要性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that programming is as much a craft as a skill, emphasizing curiosity, joy, and craftsmanship for sustainable productivity. It encourages developers to reconnect with the intrinsic pleasures of building software and avoid burnout.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张编程不仅是技能，更是一门工艺；强调好奇心、乐趣和匠心对于长期产出的重要性。鼓励开发者重新寻找编程的内在乐趣，避免职业倦怠。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://calabro.io/ohbf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>One Hash Bloom Filters</span><span class='zh'>单哈希布隆过滤器</span></a><span class="dom">calabro.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An exploration of a Bloom filter variant that uses a single hash function, reducing memory and computation while preserving useful false-positive rates.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章提出一种只需单一哈希函数即可实现的布隆过滤器变体，进一步降低内存和计算开销，同时保持可控的误报率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An exploration of a Bloom filter variant that uses a single hash function, reducing memory and computation while preserving useful false-positive rates.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章提出一种只需单一哈希函数即可实现的布隆过滤器变体，进一步降低内存和计算开销，同时保持可控的误报率。适用于资源受限的系统中高效的集合成员资格检测，并讨论了权衡与应用场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deathplanswitch.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Death Plan Switch, a self-hosted dead man&#x27;s switch for solo devs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Claude Code 的 Lights-on 软件工厂样板</span></a><span class="dom">deathplanswitch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Death Plan Switch offers a self-hosted dead-man&#x27;s switch for solo developers to securely hand off critical data, keys, or code to trusted contacts if they become unavailable.</span><span class='zh'>Death Plan Switch 为独立开发者提供一个自托管的死线开关，方便在无法工作时把关键数据、密钥或代码安全移交给信任的联系人。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Death Plan Switch offers a self-hosted dead-man&#x27;s switch for solo developers to securely hand off critical data, keys, or code to trusted contacts if they become unavailable. It provides a privacy-preserving, local-first workflow that avoids relying on closed, external services.</span><span class='zh'>Death Plan Switch 为独立开发者提供一个自托管的死线开关，方便在无法工作时把关键数据、密钥或代码安全移交给信任的联系人。它采用隐私保护、本地优先的工作流，尽量避免对外部服务的依赖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cxdd8mm6qy4mo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Revolut down as users report app not working</span><span class='zh'>Revolut 遭遇宕机，部分用户无法使用应用</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Revolut experienced an outage with users reporting the app not functioning and some services disrupted.</span><span class='zh'>Revolut 出现故障，用户反映应用无法使用、部分服务受影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Revolut experienced an outage with users reporting the app not functioning and some services disrupted. The incident underscores fintech platforms&#x27; reliance on cloud backends and the importance of robust incident response and status signals.</span><span class='zh'>Revolut 出现故障，用户反映应用无法使用、部分服务受影响。这类金融科技平台对云端后端的依赖凸显了容量与灾备的重要性，以及对稳定性与状态披露的要求。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Laminar, a RL-trained model from YC S24, identifies errors in agent traces, enabling faster debugging of RL systems.</span><span class='zh'>Laminar 是 YC S24 的强化学习训练模型，能够在代理轨迹中发现错误。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Laminar, a RL-trained model from YC S24, identifies errors in agent traces, enabling faster debugging of RL systems. It helps reduce manual inspection and improve reliability, though it raises questions about training data quality and coverage.</span><span class='zh'>Laminar 是 YC S24 的强化学习训练模型，能够在代理轨迹中发现错误。该工具有望加速 RL 系统的调试、减少人工检查，但也带来关于训练数据质量与覆盖范围的考虑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-025-58290-8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Restoration of Retinal Regenerative Potential (2025)</span><span class='zh'>视网膜再生潜力的修复（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">nature.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2025 study reports restoration of retinal regenerative potential, showing molecular cues that reactivate dormant regeneration pathways in retinal cells.</span><span class='zh'>一项 2025 年的研究报道视网膜再生潜力的恢复，揭示能重新激活视网膜细胞中潜伏再生通路的分子线索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2025 study reports restoration of retinal regenerative potential, showing molecular cues that reactivate dormant regeneration pathways in retinal cells. If reproducible, this could inform therapies for degenerative diseases like AMD by promoting endogenous repair rather than implants.</span><span class='zh'>一项 2025 年的研究报道视网膜再生潜力的恢复，揭示能重新激活视网膜细胞中潜伏再生通路的分子线索。若能在更广泛的模型中复现，可能为退行性视网膜疾病如黄斑变性提供以自体修复为核心的治疗路线。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astronomers study a trio of dim, ghostly galaxies that challenge and refine dark matter models.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家研究三座暗淡的“幽灵”星系，这组观测对暗物质模型提出挑战并提供更精确的约束。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astronomers study a trio of dim, ghostly galaxies that challenge and refine dark matter models. The unusual dynamics in these faint systems help constrain which properties dark matter could or could not have.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家研究三座暗淡的“幽灵”星系，这组观测对暗物质模型提出挑战并提供更精确的约束。它们的异常动力学有助于界定暗物质可能具备或不具备的性质。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New findings suggest ovaries stay metabolically active after menopause, challenging assumptions about postmenopausal physiology.</span><span class='zh'>新发现表明更年期后卵巢仍具代谢活性，挑战了对绝经后生理的传统认知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New findings suggest ovaries stay metabolically active after menopause, challenging assumptions about postmenopausal physiology. The study opens questions about aging, hormone regulation, and potential therapeutic angles.</span><span class='zh'>新发现表明更年期后卵巢仍具代谢活性，挑战了对绝经后生理的传统认知。研究引发对衰老、激素调控及潜在治疗方向的新问题。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ZX Spectrum Next Laptop presents a 3D‑printed, portable build of the ZX Spectrum Next, blending vintage hardware with modern fabrication.</span><span class='zh'>ZX Spectrum Next 笔记本电脑（版本 2）通过 3D 打印和硬件改造，将复古的 Spectrum Next 搭建为便携设备，展示了对复古计算和开源硬件生态的持续热情，以及将经典机器改造成笔记本的实现要点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ZX Spectrum Next Laptop presents a 3D‑printed, portable build of the ZX Spectrum Next, blending vintage hardware with modern fabrication. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://antelang.org/blog/safe_cyclic_mutability/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Borrow Checking Zero-Cost Cyclic Mutability</span><span class='zh'>借用检查：零成本的循环可变性</span></a><span class="dom">antelang.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Describes a borrow-checking design that supports cyclic mutability with zero cost, enabling safe cyclic data structures without a garbage collector.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一种零成本的借用检查设计，支持循环可变性，能够在无垃圾回收的情况下安全实现循环数据结构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Describes a borrow-checking design that supports cyclic mutability with zero cost, enabling safe cyclic data structures without a garbage collector. The approach aims to combine Rust‑like safety with the ergonomics of mutable cycles, preserving performance. If validated, it could broaden what you can model without sacrificing speed.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一种零成本的借用检查设计，支持循环可变性，能够在无垃圾回收的情况下安全实现循环数据结构。该思路力求在保持 Rust 式安全性的同时，提供可变循环的易用性与高性能。如果落地，或将扩展在不牺牲性能前提下的建模能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.quantamagazine.org/how-do-fungi-keep-the-planet-healthy-20261015/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How Do Fungi Keep the Planet Healthy?</span><span class='zh'>真菌如何让地球保持健康？</span></a><span class="dom">quantamagazine.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fungi play crucial roles as decomposers, symbionts, and biotechnological agents.</span><span class='zh'>真菌在分解、共生关系和生物技术应用中发挥关键作用，支撑养分循环、植物健康与生态系统对气候变化的韧性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fungi play crucial roles as decomposers, symbionts, and biotechnological agents. They support nutrient cycling, plant health, and ecosystem resilience against climate change, through networks and metabolic processes.</span><span class='zh'>真菌在分解、共生关系和生物技术应用中发挥关键作用，支撑养分循环、植物健康与生态系统对气候变化的韧性。文中介绍了菌丝网络在土壤健康与碳循环中的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gardinerbryant.com/what-if-video-games-came-on-vhs/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Porting video games to VHS</span><span class='zh'>将电子游戏移植到 VHS</span></a><span class="dom">gardinerbryant.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece imagines porting video games to VHS tapes, exploring how game logic could be encoded into video sequences and the nostalgia-versus-technical limits of such a format.</span><span class='zh'>文章设想将电子游戏移植到 VHS 影像带，探讨如何将游戏逻辑编码进影像序列，以及此格式带来的怀旧情怀与技术局限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece imagines porting video games to VHS tapes, exploring how game logic could be encoded into video sequences and the nostalgia-versus-technical limits of such a format.</span><span class='zh'>文章设想将电子游戏移植到 VHS 影像带，探讨如何将游戏逻辑编码进影像序列，以及此格式带来的怀旧情怀与技术局限。</span></p>
